Motivation: 缩小当前开源文生图模型与领先闭源系统之间的性能差距，通过更有效的大规模预训练数据挖掘和改进的训练监督质量来实现。

Method: 预训练采用自底向上的数据构建流程（细粒度图像分类、丰富字幕标注、美学评估、分层采样）；后训练采用自顶向下的数据构建流程，结合多样化提示标注和稳定的DPO策略进行人类美学偏好对齐；进一步通过MT-DMD缓解蒸馏中的能力漂移，训练了8-NFE的Turbo版本，并配备了轻量级Prompt Enhancer将简洁用户意图扩展为结构化视觉描述。

Result: 在定性及定量实验中，ERNIE-Image在开源模型中达到领先性能，在指令跟随、文本渲染和美学质量上接近顶级商业模型。

Conclusion: ERNIE-Image有效弥合了开源与闭源文生图模型之间的差距，并将开源模型及美学资源以促进学术研究和技术进步。

Abstract: We introduce ERNIE-Image, an open-source text-to-image generation model built upon an 8B single-stream DiT architecture. ERNIE-Image aims to bridge the gap between current open-source models and leading closed-source systems through more effective mining of large-scale pre-training data and improved supervision quality throughout training. During pre-training, we adopt a bottom-up data construction pipeline that combines fine-grained image categorization, rich caption annotation, aesthetic assessment, and hierarchical sampling. This strategy reduces data noise while preserving long-tail concepts and detailed real-world knowledge, providing a stronger foundation for complex generation tasks. In the post-training stage, we use a top-down data construction pipeline for high-demand scenarios, diversify prompt annotations to better match real user inputs, and apply a stabilized DPO strategy to align the model with human aesthetic preferences. We further train ERNIE-Image-Turbo for efficient 8-NFE generation and propose MT-DMD to mitigate capability drift during distillation. To make the model easier to use in practical scenarios, we equip it with a lightweight Prompt Enhancer that expands concise user intents into structured visual descriptions. In addition, we develop ERNIE-Image-Aes, an industrial-grade aesthetic model, together with ERNIE-Image-Aes-1K, a human-annotated benchmark for realistic aesthetic evaluation. Extensive qualitative and quantitative experiments show that ERNIE-Image achieves leading performance among open-source models and approaches top-tier commercial models in instruction following, text rendering, and aesthetic quality. We release the trained models and aesthetic resources to facilitate further academic research and technical progress in the AIGC community.

</details>


### [109] [Towards Reliable Fetal Ultrasound Interpretation with Multi-Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25357)
*Xiaotian Hu,Mingxuan Liu,Junwei Huang,Kasidit Anmahapong,Yifei Chen,Yiming Huang,Xuguang Bai,Zihan Li,Hongjia Yang,Yingqi Hao,Hong Xu,Yu Jiang,Tian Tian,Yi Liao,Haibo Qu,Qiyuan Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FetUSAgents，一个工具增强的多智能体系统，用于全面胎儿超声解读，通过协作LLM智能体和双路径证据仲裁机制，在VQA任务上比最强基线提升超25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有"一任务一模型"范式限制了多步骤流程中的证据系统整合，多模态大模型在胎儿超声分析中存在领域知识不足和幻觉风险。

Method: 提出FetUSAgents多智能体系统，包含任务特定视觉工具、LLM协作智能体、双路径证据仲裁（DPEA）机制和检索增强证据库；并构建FetUS-VQA基准数据集。

Result: 在跨域实验上，FetUSAgents在VQA准确率上超越最强基线超过25%，优于通用和医学多模态大模型。

Conclusion: 该方法为产前影像的循证临床助手提供了一条可扩展的路径。

Abstract: Automated fetal ultrasound interpretation requires a workflow from visual perception, including plane recognition and anatomical segmentation, to clinical understanding, including biometric measurement and diagnostic reporting. However, the prevailing "one-task, one-model" paradigm limits systematic integration of evidence across this multi-step process. Although multimodal large language models (MLLMs) show promising visual understanding, their limited domain-specific grounding and hallucination risks restrict reliability in fetal ultrasound analysis. To address these limitations, we propose FetUSAgents, a tool-augmented multi-agent system for comprehensive fetal ultrasound interpretation, supporting visual question answering (VQA), report generation, image captioning, and video summarization. FetUSAgents coordinates task-specific visual tools through collaborative LLM agents and decomposes clinical queries into subtasks that progress from anatomical recognition to quantitative measurement. We further introduce Dual-Path Evidence Arbitration (DPEA), which integrates LLM-based deliberative reasoning with structured computational evidence from specialized visual tools. A retrieval-enhanced evidence bank consolidates intermediate findings to support traceable and clinically grounded conclusions. In addition, we construct FetUS-VQA, a dedicated VQA benchmark for fetal ultrasound, comprising 1,892 images and 3,205 question-answer pairs across 10 clinical tasks. Extensive out-of-distribution experiments show that FetUSAgents outperforms general and medical MLLMs, exceeding the strongest baseline by more than 25 percent in VQA accuracy. These results suggest a scalable route toward evidence-driven clinical assistants for prenatal imaging. Code is available.

</details>


### [110] [MARVEL: Universal Murray's Law-informed Vessel Tree Segmentation and Topology Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25363)
*Yi Zhou,Thiara Sana Ahmed,Jacqueline Chua,Meng Wang,Qinrong Zhang,Alejandro F. Frangi,Huazhu Fu,Jun Cheng,Leopold Schmetterer,Bingyao Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MARVEL框架，将Murray定律等生物物理约束以可微正则化形式融入血管分割深度学习模型，在8个公开数据集上验证了其在分割精度、拓扑一致性和生理合理性上的优越性，并证明其能提升基于血管拓扑的高血压临床分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习方法进行血管分割时忽视了Murray定律等生物物理约束，导致分割结果出现生理学上不合理的分支结构和血管树误分类，使得自动化分割结果不可靠，无法用于下游血流模拟或疾病量化等临床任务。

Method: MARVEL是一个主干无关(backbone-agnostic)的框架，通过像素级监督结合显式半径预测，利用经验宽度-指数映射导出局部分叉约束，并将这些约束设计为可微正则化项在训练过程中引导模型生成生理一致的重建结果。

Result: 在8个公开数据集、多种血管模态和分割主干上的评估表明，MARVEL在分割精度、拓扑一致性和生理合理性上均表现优越。在高血压分类任务中，通过动静脉压力差显著提升分类效果(p<0.001)，优于基线模型。

Conclusion: 融入生物物理先验的血管分割方法能够生成更可靠的血管树结构，不仅在分割指标上有所提升，更重要的是能够保留病理性的血管狭窄和拓扑连接性，从而在下游临床任务中提供显著的预测价值。

Abstract: Vascular circulation follows fundamental biophysical principles that optimize mass transport and metabolic energy expenditure, which can be effectively modeled by Murray's law. However, contemporary deep learning methods for vascular segmentation often neglect these biophysical constraints. This leads to physiologically implausible branching and misclassification vascular trees, rendering. These automated segmentation results are unreliable unreliable for downstream clinical tasks such as blood flow simulation or disease quantification. In this paper, we introduce MARVEL (Universal MurrAy's law-infoRmed Vessel sEgmentation and topoLogy estimation), a backbone-agnostic framework that integrates biophysical priors into vascular tree extraction. MARVEL combines per-pixel supervision with explicit radius predictions to enforce local bifurcation constraints derived from an empirical width-exponent mapping. We implement these constraints as differentiable regularizers during training to guide models toward physiologically consistent reconstructions. We evaluate MARVEL on eight public datasets across multiple vascular modalities and segmentation backbones. Results demonstrate MARVEL's superior performance in segmentation accuracy, topological consistency, and physiological plausibility. By converting segmented masks into graph-based hemodynamic simulations, we demonstrate that MARVEL preserves the subtle pathological narrowing and topological connectivity required to distinguish hypertensive from normotensive eyes. Results show that MARVEL significantly improves the classification of hypertension via arteriovenous pressure differences in the eye (p < 0.001), outperforming baseline models in both topological consistency and clinical predictive value.

</details>


### [111] [Can MLLMs Reason Beyond Language? VisReason: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Vision-Centric Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25364)
*Longteng Guo,Yifan Wang,Pengkang Huo,Tailai Chen,Yuze Wu,Jing Liu,Xinxin Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VisReason是一个专注于视觉中心推理的基准测试，包含1,505个日常场景问题，涵盖感知、结构和概念推理三类10个类别，旨在评估多模态大模型在视觉证据基础上的推理能力。实验表明该基准揭示了人类与当前MLLM之间的显著差距，且测试时推理策略带来的收益有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在视觉推理基准上表现强劲，但模型表现是否真正基于视觉证据尚不明确。作者旨在构建一个视觉信息与推理紧密耦合的基准，以严格诊断模型的视觉中心推理能力，排除语言捷径的干扰。

Method: 构建VisReason基准，包含1,505个高质量问题，覆盖10个类别，分为感知推理、结构推理和概念推理三大层次。采用人工标注与多轮审核确保质量，并在多个主流MLLM上进行系统评估，同时引入人类表现作为基线。

Result: VisReason对人类与MLLM的性能差距显著放大；现有MLLM在该基准上的准确率远低于人类；增加测试时计算（如思维链、多数投票等策略）带来的性能提升有限，表明该基准对当前模型构成实质性挑战。

Conclusion: VisReason是一个有效的视觉中心推理诊断工具，揭示了当前MLLM在真正基于视觉证据的推理方面的本质不足，为未来改进提供了明确方向。

Abstract: Recent multimodal large language models (MLLMs) achieve strong performance on visual reasoning benchmarks, yet it remains unclear to what extent such performance reflects reasoning directly grounded in visual evidence. We introduce VisReason, a benchmark for vision-centric reasoning in everyday scenarios where perception and inference are tightly coupled. VisReason contains 1,505 questions across 10 categories spanning perceptual, structural, and conceptual reasoning. Our evaluation shows that VisReason poses a qualitatively different challenge from existing benchmarks, exposing substantial gaps between humans and current MLLMs and revealing limited benefits from test-time reasoning strategies. VisReason offers a focused diagnostic for evaluating vision-centric reasoning beyond language.

</details>


### [112] [Physics-Aware 3D Gaussian Editing for Driving Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25373)
*Feng Zhou,Jian Zhang,Yuhang Sun,He Wang,Qiong Wen,Debao Kong,Tieru Wu,Rui Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RoVES是一种面向自动驾驶场景的物理感知3D高斯泼溅编辑系统，支持单图像驱动的道路几何插入（如减速带、凹陷路面）和4-DOF半车动力学模型耦合的车辆姿态校正，实现无需优化的道路编辑（1.84秒）和完整的6.24秒管线处理，在Waymo数据集上验证了效率和视觉一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS场景编辑方法对道路几何编辑支持有限（如插入减速带或凹陷路面），且缺乏车辆-道路交互动力学耦合。这类编辑对生成极端驾驶场景的训练数据和评估系统可靠性至关重要，但现有方法要么需要分钟级优化，要么只能处理外观或物体级编辑，无法实现物理感知的道路不规则性编辑。

Method: RoVES提出单图像驱动的道路几何插入方法（无需优化，1.84秒完成），并将编辑后的道路剖面与4-DOF半车动力学模型耦合，实现车辆在垂直位移和俯仰角上的物理感知姿态校正。完整管线包括颜色迁移和车辆动力学姿态校正，耗时6.24秒，通过逐帧姿态编辑近似动力学一致的垂向响应。

Result: 在Waymo数据集上的实验表明，RoVES在物理感知驾驶场景生成中提供了实用的效率和具有竞争力的视觉一致性。

Conclusion: RoVES通过单次前向的道路几何编辑与动力学模型耦合，有效解决了现有方法在道路不规则性编辑和车辆-道路交互建模上的不足，为自动驾驶极端场景的数据生成提供了高效且物理一致的解决方案。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has shown great potential in autonomous driving simulation and data generation, enabling photorealistic reconstruction and flexible scene manipulation. However, existing 3DGS scene editing methods have limited support for road geometry editing (e.g., inserting speed humps or sunken roads), and generally do not couple such edits with plausible vehicle-road interaction dynamics. Such editing is essential for generating training data under extreme driving scenarios or evaluating system reliability under these road irregularities. Moreover, many optimization-based methods require minutes of per-edit refinement, while existing efficient alternatives mainly focus on appearance-level or object-level manipulation rather than physics-aware road irregularity editing. To address these limitations, we propose RoVES, a Road-and-Vehicle Editing System for physics-aware 3D Gaussian editing in driving scenes. RoVES enables single-image-driven road geometry insertion and couples the edited road profile with a 4-DOF half-car vehicle dynamics model to achieve physics-aware vehicle pose correction in vertical displacement and pitch. RoVES inserts road elements in a one-shot, optimization-free pipeline (1.84s), and the full pipeline (including color transfer and vehicle-dynamics-based pose correction) completes in 6.24s; it edits dynamic vehicles via pose editing and corrects poses frame-by-frame to approximate dynamics-consistent vertical displacement and pitch responses. Experiments on the Waymo dataset show that RoVES provides practical efficiency and competitive visual consistency for physics-aware driving scene generation.

</details>


### [113] [Adversarial Orthogonal Disentanglement for LVLM Hallucination Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25377)
*Ruoxi Cheng,Haoxuan Ma,Zhengfei Hai,Yiyan Huang,Ranjie Duan,Tianle Zhang,Xu Yang,Ziyi Ye,Xingjun Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出对抗性正交解缠（AOD）框架，通过学习幻觉相关方向并利用对比解码策略，有效抑制LVLM中的幻觉问题，同时保持通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM存在幻觉问题（生成内容与视觉事实冲突），现有方法依赖昂贵的外部干预或受限于有缺陷的注意力权重和纠缠的隐藏表示。

Method: 提出AOD框架：通过极小极大目标学习幻觉相关方向——分类器将幻觉信号集中到投影分量，对手通过梯度反转层从正交残差空间中移除幻觉信号；利用学习到的方向实现无需训练的双前向对比解码策略。

Result: 在3个LVLM、4个幻觉基准和4个实用基准上，AOD一致优于强基线：POPE准确率平均提升6%以上，AMBER提升6%，在MMMU等实用任务上保持强性能。跨数据集鲁棒迁移表明AOD捕获了通用幻觉偏差。

Conclusion: AOD是一种有效的隐空间几何框架，能够在不牺牲通用能力的前提下缓解LVLM幻觉问题，且具有良好的泛化性。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have advanced multimodal understanding, yet their reliability is limited by hallucination, where generated content conflicts with visual facts. Existing mitigation methods either rely on costly external interventions, such as instruction tuning and retrieval, or use internal mechanisms that remain limited by flawed attention weights and entangled hidden representations. We propose Adversarial Orthogonal Disentanglement (AOD), a latent geometric framework for mitigating LVLM hallucinations. AOD learns a hallucination-related direction through a minimax objective: a classifier concentrates hallucination signals into the projected component, while an adversary removes them from the orthogonal residual space via a Gradient Reversal Layer. The learned direction enables a training-free dual-forward-pass contrastive decoding strategy that suppresses hallucinations while preserving general capabilities. Experiments on three LVLMs across four hallucination and four utility benchmarks show that AOD consistently outperforms strong baselines. It improves POPE accuracy by over 6\% on average, boosts AMBER by 6\%, and maintains strong performance on utility tasks such as MMMU. Further analysis shows robust transfer across datasets, suggesting that AOD captures general hallucination-related biases rather than dataset-specific artifacts. Our source code and datasets are available at https://github.com/Hunter-Wrynn/AOD.

</details>


### [114] [CollectionLoRA: Collecting 50 Effects in 1 LoRA via Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25378)
*Fangtai Wu,Hailong Guo,Shijie Huang,Jiayi Song,Yubo Huang,Mushui Liu,Zhao Wang,Yunlong Yu,Jiaming Liu,Ruihua Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CollectionLoRA提出多教师同策略蒸馏框架，将多达50种不同效果LoRA和少步生成能力蒸馏到单个LoRA中，通过概率双流路由、非对称正交提示和从粗到精蒸馏目标解决参数干扰和概念泄露问题，显著降低部署开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有定制化图像编辑方法中，随着所需效果数量增加，存储和动态加载多个效果LoRA导致部署开销剧增，且级联加速模块时引发参数干扰，导致概念泄露和风格退化。

Method: 提出CollectionLoRA框架包含三个核心组件：(i) 概率双流路由机制，训练时随机切换数据源以增强泛化性；(ii) 非对称正交提示策略，在提示空间实现概念隔离；(iii) 从粗到精蒸馏目标，缓解师生模型间的分布差距。

Result: 实验证明CollectionLoRA能将所有定制化效果和少步生成能力蒸馏到单个LoRA中，在降低部署开销的同时，概念保真度达到或超过独立训练的教师模型。

Conclusion: CollectionLoRA从根本上解决了多效果LoRA融合中的特征干扰问题，大幅降低部署成本，同时保持高质量的概念保真度。

Abstract: Customized image editing aims to equip pre-trained diffusion models with specific visual effects using limited paired data, typically via Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA). As the number of desired effects grows, storing and dynamically loading numerous these effect LoRAs significantly increases deployment overhead. Furthermore, current pipelines typically cascade these effect LoRAs with acceleration modules for fast generation, which triggers severe parameter interference and results in concept bleeding and style degradation. We propose CollectionLoRA, a multi-teacher on-policy distillation framework capable of distilling the concepts of up to 50 different effect LoRAs along with few-step generation capabilities into a single LoRA. This fundamentally resolves the feature interference issue and significantly reduces deployment costs. Specifically, the method introduces (i) a Probabilistic Dual-Stream Routing mechanism that enables the model to randomly switch between data sources during training, effectively enhancing its generalization in unseen scenarios; (ii) an Asymmetric Orthogonal Prompting strategy to achieve concept isolation within the prompt space; (iii) a Coarse-to-Fine Distillation Objective to mitigate the distribution gap between the teacher and student models. Extensive evaluations show that CollectionLoRA distills all customized effects and few-step generation into a single LoRA, reducing deployment overhead while achieving concept fidelity comparable to or better than independently trained teacher models.

</details>


### [115] [Subspace-Guided Semantic and Topological Invariant Registration for Annotation-Free Ultrasound Plane Quality Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25396)
*Chunzheng Zhu,Jianxin Lin,Feng Wang,Cheng Jiang,Guanghua Tan,Zhenyu Zhou,Shengli Li,Kenli Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: STRIQ是一个无需标注的超声图像质量控制框架，通过潜空间配准对齐器和正交知识子空间模块，将质量控制重新定义为子空间引导的一致性度量问题，在US4QA和CAMUS数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超声图像质量控制方法过度依赖逐平面标注，或使用伪标签方法但易受临床采集中的空间形变导致的系统性偏差影响，亟需一种无需标注、鲁棒的质控方案。

Method: 提出STRIQ框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 潜空间配准对齐器(LRA)，建立查询图像与方差驱动锚点之间的层次化特征空间对应关系，锚点通过方差谱准则从无标注数据中自主蒸馏得到；(2) 正交知识子空间(OKS)模块，将平面特定表示分解为相互正交的子空间，实现细粒度专家协作并防止平面间干扰。

Result: 在内部US4QA和公开CAMUS数据集上，STRIQ与临床质量评分的相关性达到SOTA水平。

Conclusion: STRIQ建立了无需标注、实时可靠的超声质量控制新范式。

Abstract: Reliable quality control (QC) of ultrasound images is essential for both real-time acquisition guidance and retrospective clinical audit, yet existing approaches rely heavily on per-plane annotations, or employ pseudo-labeling prone to systematic bias under spatial deformations inherent in clinical acquisition. We present STRIQ, a registration-driven framework that recasts annotation-free US plane quality control as a subspace-guided consistency measurement problem. Specifically, STRIQ introduces a Latent Registration Aligner (LRA) to establish hierarchical feature space correspondences between query images and variance-driven anchors, which are autonomously distilled from unlabeled data via a variance spectrum criterion to serve as structurally stable prototypes. To further disambiguate anatomical planes and mitigate negative knowledge transfer, we propose an Orthogonal Knowledge Subspace (OKS) module. The OKS decomposes plane-specific representations into mutually orthogonal subspaces, enabling fine-grained expert collaboration while preventing inter-plane interference, ensuring that the quality metric is grounded in principled subspace proximity. Extensive experiments on the in-house US4QA and public CAMUS datasets demonstrate that STRIQ achieves state-of-the-art correlation with clinical quality scores, establishing a new paradigm for annotation-free, real-time reliable ultrasound quality control. Our code is available at https://github.com/zhcz328/STRIQ.

</details>


### [116] [Anatomy-Anchored Self-Supervision: Distilling Vision Foundation Models for Invariant Ultrasound Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25402)
*Chunzheng Zhu,Yijun Wang,Jianxin Lin,Feng Wang,Hongwei Wang,Lei Zhao,Shengli Li,Kenli Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ANAUS框架，通过锚定解剖结构实现超声图像的自监督表征学习，利用LP-SAM模块实现免标注解剖分割，并设计双策略自监督范式（跨视图语义感知解剖分离对齐+上下文核心区域预测），在6个公开数据集上超越现有SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超声图像自监督预训练方法仅在图像或帧级别操作，忽略了与临床对齐的解剖上下文信息，无法学习到具有临床意义的表征。

Method: 提出ANAUS框架：(1) 利用可学习潜在提示引擎+一次性域适应，构建LP-SAM模块实现免标注大规模解剖分割；(2) 双策略自监督学习范式：跨视图语义感知解剖分离对齐（同一解剖区域特征不变性+不同结构间可区分性）和上下文核心区域预测（重建被破坏区域以捕获细粒度结构细节）。

Result: 在6个公开数据集上，ANAUS一致超越当前SOTA方法，同时保持临床部署所需的计算效率。

Conclusion: 锚定解剖结构的自监督学习范式能够有效提升超声图像表征的临床对齐性和迁移能力，且无需额外标注成本。

Abstract: Self-supervised pre-training paradigm has gained increasing prominence for learning transferable representations in medical imaging, yet existing methods for ultrasound (US) images operate at the image or frame level, overlooking the anatomical context for clinical-aligned representation learning. In this work, we propose an anatomy-anchored ultrasound self-supervision framework ANAUS that shifts representation learning from generic visual regions to clinically meaningful anatomical structures. Utilizing a learnable latent prompt engine alongside a one-time domain adaptation on existing public image--mask pairs, we empower the LP-SAM module to achieve annotation-free anatomy delineation at scale. Building upon this anatomical grounding, we propose a dual-policy self-supervised learning paradigm consisting of inter-view semantics-aware anatomy-separating alignment and contextual core-region prediction to enhance representation learning. Specifically, the former enforces feature invariance within identical anatomical regions while promoting discriminability across distinct structures; the latter compels the model to reconstruct corrupted regions, thereby capturing fine-grained structural details. Extensive evaluations on six public datasets demonstrate that \ours{} consistently outstrips current state-of-the-art methods while maintaining the computational efficiency essential for clinical deployment. Code is available at https://github.com/zhcz328/ANAUS.

</details>


### [117] [Towards Active Real-to-Twin Inspection: A New Paradigm for Zero-Shot Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25407)
*Jiaxuan Liu,Yunkang Cao,Yufeng Chen,Chunyang Li,Yuhuan Du,Hui Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Real-to-Twin异常检测新任务，通过AVATAR框架实现零样本异常检测，利用无缺陷的真实-数字孪生对进行语义对齐，在动态视角下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本异常检测方法依赖被动、固定视角的2D图像，无法适应真实工业检测中主动、动态的观测需求，严重制约了在具身工业检测中的应用。

Method: 提出AVATAR框架，通过无缺陷的真实图像与CAD数字孪生配对，学习鲁棒的语义对齐，弥合良性的Sim2Real域间隙，将CAD先验转化为动态的无异常参考，以不可对齐偏差形式零样本定位各类异常。

Result: 大量实验表明AVATAR显著优于改编的当前最优基线方法，对严重的视角变化具有出色的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过将物理观测与几何匹配的CAD数字孪生直接对比，AVATAR有效突破了传统固定视角2D图像的局限性，为工业检测中的零样本异常检测提供了新范式。

Abstract: The deployment of zero-shot anomaly detection (AD) in embodied industrial inspection is severely bottlenecked by its reliance on passive, fixed-viewpoint 2D imagery. Such formulations inherently fail to accommodate the active, dynamic observations required in real-world environments. To break this limitation, we introduce Real-to-Twin Anomaly Detection, a novel task that evaluates physical observations directly against geometrically matched CAD Digital Twins. To tackle this new task, we propose AVATAR, a framework designed to learn robust semantic alignment between Real and Digital Twins. By bridging benign Sim2Real domain gaps using only defect-free pairs, AVATAR effectively transforms CAD priors into dynamic, anomaly-free references. This elegant formulation enables the model to localize diverse anomalies in a zero-shot manner as unalignable deviations, eliminating the need for defect annotations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AVATAR substantially outperforms adapted state-of-the-art baselines, exhibiting exceptional robustness to severe viewpoint variations. The code and dataset will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [118] [Generating 3D models from sketches of human faces using a combined approach of Convolutional Neural Networks, Procedural Modeling, and Contour Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25418)
*Nancy Iskander*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合CNN、参数化3D人脸模型（Valley Girl）和Active Snake Contours的新方法，首次实现从人脸素描中检测表情并生成对应3D模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人脸表情显著改变面部轮廓，现有方法未充分利用表情信息来辅助3D模型生成。

Method: 1) 用CNN在自建数据集上检测素描中的FACS动作单元以识别表情；2) 将检测到的表情映射到参数化3D人脸模型Valley Girl上；3) 使用Active Snake Contours寻找变换参数，缩小模型与素描之间的差距。

Result: 首次在文献中实现从素描中检测表情并生成对应表情的3D人脸模型。

Conclusion: 结合表情检测与3D生成的方法能有效从素描创建具有正确表情的3D人脸模型。

Abstract: Generating 3D models from face sketches is an active topic of research in Computer Graphics due to its potential to tremendously facilitate the modeling of faces for both professional 3D arists and novices. Motivated by the observation that facial expressions are responsible for significantly altering and shaping the contours in our faces, we combine both expression detection and 3D model generation in our approach. The result is a novel approach to generating 3D models from sketches which relies on three components: Convolutional Neural Networks, a parametric 3D face model (Valley Girl), and Active Snake Contours. For the first time in the literature, CNNs are trained (using our own generated dataset) to detect the expression in the given sketch through detecting the active FACS Action Units. The expression is then duplicated on Valley Girl to obtain a 3D model with a similar expression. Active Snake Contours are then used to find the transforms needed to close the gaps between that model and the given sketch.

</details>


### [119] [Binding Visual Features Point by Point](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25427)
*Udith Haputhanthri,Declan Campbell,Rim Assouel,Jonathan D. Cohen,Taylor W. Webb*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了通过文本指向（pointing-via-text）训练可以诱导视觉语言模型内部产生类似人类视觉的串行搜索机制，从而解决多目标场景中的绑定问题（binding problem），并实现组合泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在多目标场景中表现不佳，存在绑定问题（无法准确将物体特征与位置对应），而人类视觉系统通过串行处理（逐次关注单个物体）解决此问题。作者希望探究指向（pointing）这一方法为何能提升模型性能，其背后的机制是否与人类串行视觉处理类似。

Method: 通过文本指向训练诱导模型产生内部视觉搜索机制，并利用微调将指向行为泛化到新任务，从机制和表征层面分析该方法如何消除绑定错误。

Result: 学习指向训练会诱导出模型内部的视觉搜索程序；指向行为可通过微调泛化到新任务；此方法消除了绑定错误并实现了组合泛化。

Conclusion: 串行处理（通过指向实现）可以像在生物视觉中一样，解决视觉语言模型的绑定问题，为模型在多目标场景中提供了一种原理性验证方案。

Abstract: Despite success on standard benchmarks, vision language models display persistent failures on tasks involving processing of multi-object scenes, including many tasks that are relatively easy for humans. Recent work has found that these failures may stem from a basic inability to accurately bind object features in-context, a challenge that is referred to as the "binding problem" in cognitive science and neuroscience. The human visual system is thought to solve this binding problem via serial processing, attending to individual objects one at a time so as to avoid interference from other objects. Recent work has proposed "pointing" -- the use of explicit spatial coordinates to refer to objects -- as an analogous solution for vision language models, and found that it improves performance on challenging multi-object tasks. However, it is unclear $\textit{why}$ (i.e., on a mechanistic or representational level) this approach improves performance, and how directly this relates to serial processing in human vision. Here, we investigate this question. We find that learning to point-via-text induces an internal visual search routine, and we characterize the mechanisms that support this procedure. We also find that pointing behavior can be generalized to new tasks via fine-tuning, and that doing so eliminates binding errors and enables compositional generalization. These results provide a proof-of-principle that serial processing can solve the binding problem for vision language models just as it does for biological vision.

</details>


### [120] [Does Seeing More Mean Knowing More? Mono-Anchored Advantage Normalization for Multi-Source Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25437)
*Fanhu Zeng,Zhicong Luo,Zefan Wang,You Li,Chi Chen,Maosong Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MARS框架，通过将单源奖励作为动态锚点，在强化学习中显式建模多源信息增益与干扰抑制，在GRPO和DAPO上分别提升3.2%和4.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）在多源视觉推理中将多模态输入简单累加，缺乏区分信息增益与干扰的机制，导致当某个源信号占主导时性能反而不如单源推理。

Method: 提出MARS（mono-anchored multi-source reasoning）框架：将每个视觉模态建模为独立信息源，以单源奖励为动态锚点，将多源融合的信息增益显式融入优势归一化，自适应增强模态间相互促进并抑制噪声或冲突。

Result: 在GRPO和DAPO上分别取得3.2%和4.9%的性能提升，在多个数据集上验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法通过理论分析证明了在梯度估计中有效量化多源融合的信息增益，实现了模态间的一致性调控。

Abstract: Visual reasoning through reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has achieved remarkable progress. However, when dealing with multi-source inputs, existing approaches tend to treat them as a mere accumulation of information, lacking explicit mechanisms to distinguish whether integrating additional sources yields information gain or introduces interference. Therefore, they struggle to effectively model dynamic interaction when integrating multiple sources, particularly when they differ significantly in physical properties and semantics, e.g., infrared and depth, leading to inferior performance to mono-source reasoning when a certain source holds the dominant signal. To address this issue, we propose MARS, a novel mono-anchored multi-source reasoning framework that models each visual modality as an independent information source. Specifically, by treating mono-source rewards as dynamic anchors, our method explicitly incorporates the information gain introduced by multi-source fusion into advantage normalization and adaptively emphasizes mutual promotion between sources while suppressing potential noise or conflicts during RLVR. From theoretical analysis, our method effectively quantifies information gain introduced by multi-source integration in gradient estimation, enabling consistent modality regulation. Empirical results also show impressive 3.2% and 4.9% performance gains on GRPO and DAPO across diverse datasets, confirming effectiveness of our method.

</details>


### [121] [Enhancing Single-Image Facial Demorphing using Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25442)
*Nitish Shukla,Arun Ross*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于多模态大语言模型（MLLM）引导的无参考人脸去变形框架，通过耦合扩散模型直接从RGB域联合重建构成图像的两个原始身份，避免了传统方法的压缩损失和身份重叠假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人脸变形攻击检测方法仅能识别变形图像，无法恢复原始构成图像或身份，限制了其取证实用性。

Method: 利用MLLM中间层的语义嵌入作为条件信号，引导一个在RGB域中联合去噪的耦合扩散模型，同时重建两个构成人脸，实现跨身份一致性保持与细粒度感知细节保留。

Result: 消融实验表明：中间MLLM层编码了更具身份判别性的表征；RGB域去变形在严格操作点下优于潜在空间方法30-40%；完整MLLM嵌入相比原始ViT特征具有显著优势，归因于多模态预训练带来的语义结构增强。

Conclusion: 该方法通过直接利用MLLM隐状态作为条件信号，规避了有损的文本生成-重编码循环，能够关注头发、背景、面部纹理等细微视觉线索，在无参考条件下实现了高效的人脸去变形重建。

Abstract: Face recognition systems are increasingly vulnerable to morphing attacks, where a composite image is crafted to match multiple identities, enabling unauthorized access and identity fraud. Existing detection methods identify morphed images but cannot recover constituent images or identities, limiting their forensic utility. This paper presents a novel reference-free facial demorphing framework that leverages Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to guide a coupled diffusion-based reconstruction process. Our key innovation lies in extracting semantic embeddings from intermediate MLLM layers to condition the demorphing, providing high-level reasoning about facial attributes and identity cues that complement low-level pixel information. We formulate demorphing as a coupled conditional generation problem, where both constituent faces are synthesized jointly through a denoising diffusion model operating directly in the RGB domain, ensuring inter-identity consistency while preserving fine-grained perceptual details. Unlike prior approaches that rely on compressed latent representations or assume identity overlap between training and testing sets, our method bypasses lossy text generation-reencoding cycles by directly utilizing MLLM hidden states as conditioning signals, enabling the denoising network to attend to subtle visual cues such as hair, background, and facial textures. Ablation studies further reveal that middle MLLM layers encode more identity-discriminative representations, RGB-domain demorphing outperforms latent-space approaches by 30--40\% at strict operating points, and full MLLM embeddings provide substantial advantages over raw ViT features through enhanced semantic structuring from multimodal pretraining.

</details>


### [122] [Pantheon360: Taming Digital Twin Generation via 3D-Aware 360° Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25449)
*Ting-Hsuan Chen,Ying-Huan Chen,Tao Tu,Jie-Ying Lee,Cho-Ying Wu,Fangzhou Lin,Hengyuan Zhang,David Paz,Xinyu Huang,Yuliang Guo,Yu-Lun Liu,Yue Wang,Liu Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Pantheon360提出了一种基于3D感知的360°视频扩散框架，通过显式3D缓存从稀疏输入重建几何结构，实现可控的高保真数字孪生视频生成，解决了透视视频生成器视场角有限导致的跨视角不一致和时间漂移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 透视视频生成器因视场角有限，需要长轨迹或多视角轨迹，导致跨视角不一致和时间漂移问题，难以实现完整的数字孪生生成。

Method: 提出Pantheon360框架，核心是显式3D缓存（从输入重建），作为任意用户定义相机路径的几何支架，扩散模型专注于真实感纹理细化，3D缓存保证全局几何一致性。

Result: 在视觉质量和几何一致性上均达到优越性能，实现可靠且灵活的360°场景生成。

Conclusion: 360°视频生成通过全景覆盖简化轨迹设计并提供强全局上下文，结合3D感知扩散模型，是解决数字孪生生成中一致性问题的高效方案。

Abstract: Generating complete digital twins from videos requires precise camera control, global scene coverage, and strict spatial-temporal consistency constraints that remain challenging for perspective video generators due to their limited field of view (FoV). Their narrow FoV forces long or multi-view trajectories, amplifying cross-view inconsistency and temporal drift. We argue that 360° video generation offers a natural solution: panoramic coverage simplifies trajectory design and provides a strong global context for maintaining coherence. We introduce Pantheon360: Taming Digital Twin Generation via 3D-Aware 360° Video Diffusion, a controllable 360° video generation framework that synthesizes high-fidelity videos from sparse 360° inputs. The key idea is an explicit 3D Cache, reconstructed from the input, which serves as a geometric scaffold for any user-defined camera path. This allows the diffusion model to focus on photorealistic texture refinement while the 3D Cache enforces global geometric consistency. Experiments show that Pantheon360 achieves superior visual quality and unmatched geometric coherence, enabling reliable and flexible 360° scene generation for downstream simulation and digital-twin applications.

</details>


### [123] [MetaphorVU: Towards Metaphorical Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25461)
*Zhuoqun Li,Boxi Cao,Guiping Jiang,Fangrui Lv,Ruotong Pan,Jianan Wang,Xiangyu Wu,Hongyu Lin,Yaojie Lu,Yong Du,Ruyin Jia,Liyan,Tingting Gao,Han Li,Xianpei Han,Le Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个系统性隐喻视频理解基准MetaphorVU-Bench，发现当前多模态大模型在隐喻视频理解上远落后于人类水平，主要原因是跨域映射缺陷，并据此提出了基于隐喻知识图谱的推理增强框架MetaphorBoost。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐喻视频在现实场景中广泛存在，但缺乏系统性的研究，这既限制了多模态大模型的实际应用，也阻碍了对其高阶认知能力的全面评估。

Method: 构建MetaphorVU-Bench基准进行系统性评估，发现跨域映射缺陷是主要瓶颈；在此基础上构建隐喻知识图谱作为映射增强，提出推理时增强框架MetaphorBoost。

Result: 当前多模态大模型在隐喻视频理解上远落后于人类水平，MetaphorBoost框架在多个模型上实现了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 该基准、分析和方法为未来推进多模态大模型的隐喻视频理解研究提供了有价值的见解和基础。

Abstract: Metaphorical videos are prevalent across various real-world scenarios to convey complex ideas, and understanding them typically requires high-order cognitive capabilities. The lack of systematic studies on metaphorical video understanding not only constrains the real-world applicability of MLLMs but also impedes the thorough assessment of their high-order cognitive capabilities. To bridge this gap, we propose MetaphorVU-Bench, the first systematic and comprehensive benchmark dedicated to metaphorical video understanding. Through experiments, we find current MLLMs struggle with accurate metaphorical video understanding, lagging far behind human level, primarily due to defective cross-domain mapping. Motivated by this finding, we construct a metaphor knowledge graph as mapping augmentation and propose MetaphorBoost, an inference-time enhancement framework achieving consistent performance improvement. Our benchmark, analysis, and method provide useful insights and a foundation for future research on advancing MLLMs.

</details>


### [124] [MAIL++: Multi-Modal Bi-directional Agent Layer for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25479)
*Kaixiang Chen,Pengfei Fang,Hui Xue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MAIL和MAIL++，这是一种将跨模态耦合直接嵌入VLM固有计算模块的参数高效微调范式，通过在核心模块后插入轻量级agent层并引入瓶颈式桥接机制，在少样本图像分类和跨域检索任务上持续超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数高效微调方法中，跨模态耦合主要依赖外部辅助模块，导致间接、粗粒度的交互，与原始VLM结构脱节，限制了表征表达能力。

Method: 冻结VLM主干，在LayerNorm等核心模块后插入轻量级agent层以近似全微调的参数更新；提出瓶颈式文本到图像桥接联合优化跨模态的成对agent层；MAIL++进一步通过元agent层、元文本桥和元图像桥实现双向跨模态交换；推理时所有agent层可重参数化回冻结主干。

Result: 在少样本图像分类和少样本通用跨域检索任务上，MAIL和MAIL++持续超越最先进的PEFT方法。

Conclusion: 将跨模态耦合直接嵌入VLM内部计算模块是一种更有效的PEFT范式，兼顾了表征表达能力与计算效率。

Abstract: Adapting large vision-language models (VLMs) such as CLIP to downstream tasks remains challenging, as full fine-tuning is computationally prohibitive and prone to overfitting in low-data regimes. Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) alleviates these issues with lightweight prompt- or adapter-based modules, and cross-modal coupling has proven especially effective by strengthening interactions between vision and language. However, existing coupling mechanisms predominantly rely on external auxiliary modules, leading to indirect, coarse-grained interactions that are structurally decoupled from the original VLM and thus limit representational expressiveness.
  In this paper, we propose Multi-Modal Interactive Agent Layer (MAIL), a PEFT paradigm that embeds cross-modal coupling directly into the intrinsic computation modules of VLMs. MAIL freezes the backbone and inserts lightweight agent layers after core modules, such as LayerNorm, to approximate the parameter updates induced by full fine-tuning. To couple visual and textual streams at this level, we introduce a bottleneck-based text-to-image bridge that jointly optimizes paired agent layers across modalities, coordinating the adaptation of corresponding computation modules. We further present MAIL++, which enables bidirectional cross-modal exchange through a meta agent layer, a meta-text bridge, and a meta-image bridge. At inference time, all agent layers are re-parameterized into the frozen backbone, preserving the original computational efficiency. Extensive experiments on few-shot image classification and few-shot universal cross-domain retrieval demonstrate that MAIL and MAIL++ consistently outperform state-of-the-art PEFT methods.

</details>


### [125] [Test-Time Self-Adaptive Conditioning for Stable Audio-Driven Talking-Head Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25488)
*Zhicheng Zhang,Lei Wang,Yu Zhang,Yongsheng Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TT-SAC，一种无需训练的参数自由推理框架，通过将生成器输出重新编码为条件表征的反馈循环，在测试时自适应调整条件，有效解决音频驱动说话头生成中静态参考图像导致的身份漂移和时间不一致问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频驱动说话头生成方法在推理阶段依赖单一静态参考图像作为条件，导致固定身份特征与动态面部运动之间存在不匹配，引发身份漂移、时间不一致和质量下降。

Method: TT-SAC通过将生成器与其编码器组成反馈回路：将生成器自身输出重新编码，构建更符合合成序列时间动态的精细化条件表征；单步自适应即可逼近生成过程的自洽均衡，无需重训练、梯度更新或额外监督。

Result: 在多个最先进模型和基准数据集上，TT-SAC在唇音同步精度、时间连贯性、身份保持和感知保真度方面均取得一致提升。

Conclusion: TT-SAC提供了一种模型无关、免训练的策略来增强生成式视频模型，将测试时条件自适应确立为稳定音频驱动肖像动画的有效机制。

Abstract: Audio-driven talking-head generation has achieved remarkable progress with recent models such as AniTalker, FLOAT, and Sonic. Despite their success, most existing approaches rely on a single static reference image to condition the entire video generation process at inference stage. This static conditioning paradigm often creates a mismatch between fixed identity features and dynamically evolving facial motion, leading to identity drift, temporal inconsistency, and degraded perceptual quality. We introduce Test-Time Self-Adaptive Conditioning (TT-SAC), a parameter-free inference framework that enables pretrained talking-head generators to adapt their conditioning representations during inference without retraining, gradient updates, or additional supervision. Instead of treating the reference portrait as immutable, TT-SAC composes the generator with its encoder in a feedback loop: the generator's own outputs are re-encoded to construct a refined conditioning representation that better aligns with the temporal dynamics of the synthesized sequence. A single adaptation step approximates a self-consistent equilibrium of the generative process, stabilizing identity and motion across time. We further provide theoretical analysis showing that test-time conditioning adaptation reduces feature variance and improves generative stability under mild Lipschitz assumptions, while exhibiting a principled bias-variance tradeoff that governs the optimal strength of adaptation. Extensive experiments on state-of-the-art talking-head generators and benchmark datasets demonstrate consistent improvements in lip-sync accuracy, temporal coherence, identity preservation, and perceptual fidelity. TT-SAC offers a model-agnostic and training-free strategy for enhancing generative video models, establishing test-time conditioning adaptation as an effective mechanism for stabilizing audio-driven portrait animation.

</details>


### [126] [Full-4D: Generating Full-Scope 4D Scenes from a Single-View Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25500)
*Tingxi Chen,Ke Hao,Yabo Chen,Zhengxue Cheng,Rong Xie,Li Song,Haibin Huang,Chi Zhang,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种从单视角视频生成全方位4D场景的框架，通过多视角视频合成+4D重建实现，贡献包括：Real-MV-4D大规模多视角视频数据集、融合时间-视图注意力的扩散模型、以及流匹配蒸馏损失优化的4D高斯表达，在视觉质量和几何一致性上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单视角视频生成4D场景是高度不适定问题，现有方法局限于单目视频、简单3D效果或微小视角扰动，无法实现真正的全方位4D生成；同时缺乏大规模覆盖完整4D场景的同步多视角视频数据集。

Method: 1) 构建大规模多视角视频数据集Real-MV-4D提供4D监督；2) 训练融合时间-视图(T×V)注意力机制的多视角视频扩散模型，嵌入几何重投影先验和显式相机条件；3) 将合成多视角视频提升为显式4D高斯表达(4DGS)，通过流匹配蒸馏损失利用多视角先验优化新视角渲染。

Result: 在视觉保真度和几何一致性上均优于现有方法，实现从单视角视频到全方位4D场景的生成。

Conclusion: 本文提出的单视角视频转4D框架通过多视角视频合成+优化重建的端到端方案，有效解决了数据缺乏和生成质量不足的问题，为4D场景生成提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Generating 4D scenes from a single-view video is inherently ill-posed: a single viewpoint lacks the information needed to recover a complete, dynamic scene with full coverage. Existing methods are typically limited to monocular videos, simple 3D effects, or only small viewpoint perturbations around the original viewpoint, falling short of true 4D generation. Meanwhile, the lack of large-scale datasets capturing full-scope 4D scenes with synchronized multi-view videos further hinders progress in this direction. We propose a novel single-view video-to-4D framework that casts full-scope 4D generation as a multi-view video synthesis followed by optimization-based 4D reconstruction from the generated views. To instantiate this formulation end-to-end, we make three key contributions. First, we introduce Real-MV-4D, a large-scale dataset of synchronized multi-view videos captured in diverse real-world environments to provide the 4D supervision. Second, we train a multi-view video diffusion model driven by a novel fused time(T)-view(V) attention mechanism that directly embeds geometric reprojection priors and explicit camera conditioning into its view-time interactions. Unlike basic feature fusion, this direct binding strictly aligns the generation process with physical 3D priors to produce a dense, synchronized T$\times $V video grid. Third, rather than relying on non-interactive and inconsistent 2D video interpolations, we lift the synthesized multi-view videos into an explicit 4D representation (i.e. 4DGS), regularized by a Flow Matching Distillation loss that exploits the multi-view prior to improve novel-view rendering. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method outperforms existing approaches in both visual fidelity and geometric consistency, enabling full-scope 4D scene generation from single-view videos.

</details>


### [127] [Metric--Phase Fields: Decoupling Distance and Sign for Thin-Structure Reconstruction from Unoriented Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25503)
*Jiayi Kong,Xuhui Chen,Chen Zong,Fei Hou,Junhui Hou,Wenping Wang,Ying He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Metric-Phase Fields (MPFs)，一种分离度量距离与拓扑相位的隐式表示，同时克服SDF在薄结构上的局限和UDF的梯度奇异性问题，实现更鲁棒的薄壳与薄板几何重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经SDF虽能重建水密流形，但严格的内部-外部约束使其无法处理薄结构和开放边界；UDF虽支持任意几何，但在零水平集处存在梯度奇异性，阻碍优化与提取。现有方法难以兼顾两类几何的鲁棒重建。

Method: 将隐式表示解耦为无符号度量场r与光滑相位场θ，通过有界相位指示器P=tanh(βθ)提供有意义的软内外线索；设计门控度量公式与残差相位注入构建带稳定近表面梯度的有符号隐函数；相位系数β可学习，自适应控制相位过渡锐度与软符号指示器饱和程度。

Result: 在合成与扫描的薄壳、薄板形状上，MPFs比SDF方法更保真地保留薄结构与分层结构，同时比UDF方法训练更鲁棒、表面提取更可靠。

Conclusion: MPFs通过解耦度量场与相位场，有效结合了SDF与UDF的优势，为薄结构几何重建提供了更优的隐式表示方案。

Abstract: Neural Signed Distance Functions (SDFs) excel at reconstructing watertight manifolds but fail on thin structures and open boundaries due to strict inside--outside constraints. Conversely, Unsigned Distance Fields (UDFs) accommodate general geometries but suffer from gradient singularities at the zero-level set, hindering optimization and extraction. We introduce Metric--Phase Fields (MPFs), a decoupled implicit representation that separates metric proximity from topological phase. Given an unoriented point cloud, MPFs learn (i) an unsigned metric field $r$ and (ii) a smooth phase field $θ$, for which we derive a bounded phase indicator $P=\tanh(βθ)$ that provides soft inside--outside cues where they are meaningful. We couple the two fields via a gated-metric formulation with a residual phase injection to obtain a signed implicit function with stable near-surface gradients. The phase coefficient $β$ is learnable, allowing MPFs to adaptively control the sharpness of the phase transition and the degree of saturation of the soft sign indicator. Experiments on both synthetic and scanned thin-shell and thin-plate shapes demonstrate that MPFs preserve thin and layered structures more faithfully than recent SDF-based methods, while also enabling more robust training and more reliable surface extraction than UDF-based approaches. Check out \href{https://github.com/JIAYI-Scarlett/ICML2026-MPF}{MPFs-GitHub} for source code and test models.

</details>


### [128] [ProSR: Process-Shaped Spatial Reasoning for Reliable Chain-of-Thought in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25524)
*Jiangyang Li,Cong Wan,Changjie Wu,Songlin Dong,Lingjun Zhang,Linzhe Shi,Xu Wang,Zhiheng Ma,Hang Zhang,Mu Xu,Yihong Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ProSR框架，通过反事实不变性惩罚和尾部漂移惩罚，在强化学习优化中约束视觉依赖性和轨迹稳定性，解决了VLM空间推理中的虚假基础（Spurious Grounding）和尾部不稳定性（Tail Instability）问题，在多个复杂和分布外空间推理基准上提升了准确率并生成了更稳定、更依赖视觉证据的推理轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM空间推理的主流训练范式依赖结果对齐或过程模仿，缺乏对推理过程的显式约束，导致模型难以保证真实的视觉依赖和稳定的推理轨迹。

Method: 构建高质量CoT数据集覆盖多样空间现象；提出ProSR过程塑造优化框架，包含反事实不变性惩罚（强制推理依赖视觉证据而非捷径）和尾部漂移惩罚（抑制推理后期不确定性异常上升），将优化目标从答案正确性扩展到视觉依赖性和轨迹稳定性两个过程维度。

Result: 在多个复杂和分布外空间推理基准上，ProSR不仅提升了答案准确率，还生成了更稳定、更依赖视觉证据的推理轨迹。

Conclusion: 对推理过程施加显式约束（视觉依赖性和轨迹稳定性）是提升VLM空间推理可靠性的有效途径，ProSR框架在改善过程质量的同时也带来了最终答案准确率的提升。

Abstract: Reliable spatial reasoning remains a core bottleneck for vision-language models (VLMs). Existing mainstream training paradigms for spatial reasoning largely rely on outcome alignment or process imitation, lacking explicit constraints on the reasoning process, and therefore struggle to ensure genuine visual dependence and stable reasoning trajectories. In this paper, we construct a high-quality CoT dataset covering diverse spatial phenomena and diagnose the model's reasoning process, revealing two typical types of process degradation during reinforcement learning optimization: Spurious Grounding, which bypasses visual evidence, and Tail Instability, where uncertainty abnormally rises in the later stage of reasoning. To address these issues, we propose ProSR, a process-shaping optimization framework for spatial reasoning. Through a Counterfactual Invariance Penalty and a Tail Drift Penalty, ProSR extends the optimization objective from single answer correctness to two process-level dimensions: visual dependence and trajectory stability. Experiments on multiple complex and out-of-distribution spatial reasoning benchmarks show that ProSR improves answer accuracy while generating reasoning trajectories that are more stable and more dependent on visual evidence.

</details>


### [129] [Tetris: Tile-level Sampling for Efficient and High-Fidelity Video Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25538)
*Chanwut Kittivorawong,Alena Chao,Charlie Si,Alvin Cheung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Tetris 是一种面向静态视频的轨道提取系统，通过基于瓦片的polyomino数据模型实现细粒度时空剪枝，在保证5%精度损失范围内，相比先前系统最高提升17.4倍吞吐量，相比全帧参考管线最高提升68.8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨道物化系统为降低成本采用时间帧采样，但会丢失细粒度跟踪所需的帧间运动信息。在静态视频中，大部分帧区域不含感兴趣目标，且不同区域可容忍不同采样率，现有方法未能充分利用这一特性进行高效剪枝。

Method: 将视频分解为基于瓦片的polyomino数据模型，通过三个上游算子实现细粒度时空剪枝：分类器识别相关瓦片并分组为polyomino；整数线性规划在用户指定精度约束下剪枝冗余polyomino；打包器将幸存polyomino组装为画布以最小化检测器调用次数。

Result: 在7个静态视频数据集上，Tetris在5%跟踪精度损失范围内保持精度；相比先前系统，在相同精度约束下最高提升17.4倍吞吐量，相比全帧参考管线最高提升68.8倍。先前系统在7个数据集中有3个超出5%精度损失上限。

Conclusion: Tetris通过细粒度时空剪枝在静态视频中实现了高吞吐、低精度损失的轨道提取，验证了基于瓦片的polyomino模型在视频分析中的有效性。

Abstract: Track materialization converts raw video into reusable object tracks that downstream queries can run against without rerunning tracking, but extracting those tracks efficiently and with high fidelity remains expensive. Prior systems reduce cost through temporal frame sampling, erasing the inter-frame motion that fine-grained tracking requires. In stationary video, however, large portions of each frame contain no objects of interest, and the remaining regions tolerate different sampling rates. We present Tetris, a track-extraction system that decomposes videos into a tile-based polyomino data model, enabling fine-grained spatiotemporal pruning that reduces detector calls with minimal fidelity loss. Tetris runs three operators upstream of the user-provided detector: a classifier identifies relevant tiles and groups them into polyominoes, an integer linear program (ILP) prunes redundant polyominoes under a user-specified accuracy constraint, and a packer assembles the survivors into canvases that minimize detector calls. Across 7 stationary-video datasets, Tetris stays within a 5% tracking accuracy loss of a full-frame, every-frame reference pipeline, whereas prior systems exceed this bound on 3 of the 7 datasets. At this 5% bound, Tetris achieves up to 17.4x higher throughput than prior systems and up to 68.8x higher than the reference pipeline. The project page is at https://tetris-db.github.io .

</details>


### [130] [Are We Overconfident in Models and Results for Semi-Supervised 3D Medical Image Segmentation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25561)
*Jun Li,Ziwei Qin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出半监督学习领域存在双重过度自信问题（算法上将预测置信度与不确定性混淆，策略上使用测试集做验证导致过拟合），提出一种三空间校准分割框架（TCSeg），通过双轴可靠性评估引擎解耦置信度与不确定性，在特征、概率和图像空间协同纠正确认偏差，并倡导使用多轮测试协议报告最终检查点结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前半监督学习中存在双重过度自信问题：算法上主流伪标签框架将预测置信度与不确定性混为一谈导致确认偏差；策略上多个基准数据集缺少专用验证集，部分研究使用测试集进行验证导致性能虚高，后续方法为超越SOTA被迫采用相同策略，引发过拟合的军备竞赛。这引发了社区中的数值提升可能反映过拟合而非真正进步的担忧。

Method: 提出三空间校准分割框架（TCSeg），基于一个原则性的双轴可靠性评估引擎，显式地将置信度与不确定性解耦，并利用该信号在特征空间、概率空间和图像空间中协同检测和纠正确认偏差。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上，TCSeg在现有评估协议下持续提供强劲性能。同时倡导社区采用多轮测试协议报告最终检查点结果，以建立更严格且更现实的基准。

Conclusion: TCSeg有效解决了半监督分割中的双重过度自信问题，但更重要的是，论文呼吁社区反思当前评估标准，采用更严格的协议以反映真实进展。

Abstract: Semi-supervised learning has become a dominant paradigm for reducing annotation costs. However, we argue that the current progress is clouded by a twofold overconfidence problem. Algorithmically, mainstream pseudo-labeling frameworks often conflate prediction confidence with uncertainty, leading to severe confirmation bias. Strategically, since multiple benchmark datasets lack dedicated validation sets, some studies use the test set for validation as well, leading to inflated performance estimates. Subsequent methods, compelled to employ the same strategy to surpass reported SOTA, trigger an arms race of overfitting. This raises concerns that the impressive numerical gains in the community may reflect overfitting rather than genuine progress. Thus, we propose a tri-space calibrated segmentation framework founded on a principled dual-axis reliability assessment engine. It explicitly decouples confidence from uncertainty and uses this signal to detect and correct confirmation bias across feature, probability, and image spaces in a collaborative manner. Across three benchmark datasets, TCSeg consistently delivers strong performance under existing evaluation protocols. More importantly, we advocate that the community report final-checkpoint results under multiple-run protocols, thereby establishing more rigorous benchmarks with a more realistic perspective. Code will be available: github.com/DirkLiii/TCSeg.

</details>


### [131] [CodecSplat: Ultra-Compact Latent Coding for Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25563)
*Pengpeng Yu,Runqing Jiang,Qi Zhang,Dingquan Li,Jing Wang,Yulan Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CodecSplat提出了一种用于前馈式3D高斯泼溅的超紧凑潜在编码框架，通过将压缩集成到高斯生成管线中，利用结构化中间特征表示代替直接压缩不规则3D高斯基元，在DL3DV和RealEstate10K数据集上以极低码率（每场景3.37-107.77 KiB）实现了23.56-27.05 dB的PSNR，压缩效率比现有方法提升一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈式3D高斯泼溅方法虽能从稀疏视图重建可渲染的高斯基元且无需逐场景优化，但缺乏紧凑的场景表示用于存储或传输。直接应用现有3DGS压缩方法对生成的不规则高斯基元进行后处理压缩，会与内部特征到高斯的生成过程解耦，限制压缩效率。

Method: CodecSplat首先将中间2D高斯生成特征编码为熵编码的场景比特流；解码时重建潜在特征，用于预测深度和高斯参数，再映射为3D高斯基元。通过将压缩嵌入前馈式高斯生成管线，避免了对不规则3D高斯基元的低效压缩。具体实现基于深度引导的多视图特征细化和分层学习特征编解码器。

Result: 在DL3DV数据集上达到23.56-26.36 dB PSNR，每场景仅需20.00-107.77 KiB；在RealEstate10K数据集上达到24.76-27.05 dB PSNR，每场景仅需3.37-12.51 KiB。码率比压缩前馈生成的高斯基元低约一个数量级，且具有可控的率失真行为。

Conclusion: CodecSplat通过在前馈式高斯生成管线内部引入潜在编码，有效解决了前馈式3DGS缺乏紧凑表示的问题，实现了极低码率下的高质量场景重建，为3DGS的存储和传输提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: While feed-forward 3D Gaussian splatting reconstructs renderable Gaussian primitives from sparse context views without per-scene optimization, existing pipelines do not provide a compact scene representation for storage or transmission. A natural solution is to apply existing 3DGS compression methods to the generated Gaussian primitives. However, this approach operates on the final irregular 3D representation and is decoupled from the internal feature-to-Gaussian generation process, which limits compression efficiency. To address this, we introduce CodecSplat, an ultra-compact latent coding framework for feed-forward 3D Gaussian splatting. CodecSplat first encodes an intermediate 2D Gaussian-generation feature into an entropy-coded scene bitstream. At the decoder, the latent feature is reconstructed and used to predict depth and Gaussian parameters, which are then mapped to 3D Gaussian primitives. Note that, by integrating compression into the feed-forward Gaussian generation pipeline, CodecSplat avoids inefficient compression over irregular 3D Gaussian primitives and allows the codec to exploit the structured intermediate feature representation. We instantiate CodecSplat on a feed-forward Gaussian splatting backbone with depth-guided multi-view feature refinement and a hierarchical learned feature codec. On DL3DV and RealEstate10K datasets, CodecSplat achieves 23.56-26.36 dB and 24.76-27.05 dB PSNR with only 20.00-107.77 KiB and 3.37-12.51 KiB per scene, respectively. This is roughly one order of magnitude smaller than compressing feed-forward generated Gaussian primitives, while preserving controllable rate-distortion behavior.

</details>


### [132] [Rethinking Scribble-Guided Image Editing: Generalization, Instruction Adherence, and Multi-Tasking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25568)
*Mingyi Xu,Jinpeng Lin,Min Zhou,Tiezheng Ge,Ming Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对涂鸦引导的图像编辑中多任务场景性能不稳定的问题，通过实证研究发现指令级泛化（跨任务、单任务到多任务）比图像域泛化更难，并提出了三项策略（覆盖率-现实课程学习、多任务拼接、编辑聚焦损失），在VIBE基准上取得了最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有涂鸦引导图像编辑模型在多任务场景下表现不稳定，作者通过实证研究发现主要瓶颈在于对多样化编辑指令的学习不足，而非图像域差异。

Method: (a) 覆盖率-现实课程学习：两阶段流水线，先构建大规模合成指令丰富数据，再精选少量真实数据优化生成真实性；(b) 多任务拼接：近乎零成本地拼接单任务样本构建多任务训练数据，且学到的能力可泛化到非拼接图像；(c) 编辑聚焦损失：利用合成数据中的变化区域，使训练聚焦于编辑区域。

Result: 在VIBE基准上，单任务和多任务涂鸦引导图像编辑均取得显著提升，达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 指令级泛化是涂鸦引导图像编辑的核心瓶颈，所提三项策略有效解决了这一问题，且数据集和模型将开源。

Abstract: Scribble-guided image editing allows users to combine simple scribble annotations with text prompts to specify both where and how an image should be edited, enabling flexible interaction with precise spatial control. However, existing models still exhibit unstable performance under this paradigm, especially in multi-task scenarios. To improve performance, we conduct empirical studies using an open-source editing model and reveal an asymmetry in generalization: instruction-level generalization, including across editing tasks and from single-task to multi-task settings, is more challenging than image-domain generalization, such as from synthetic to real-world images or from mosaicked to regular images. This suggests that the primary bottleneck lies in insufficient learning for diverse editing instructions rather than in the image domain gap. Motivated by this insight, we propose three strategies: (a) a Coverage-then-Realism Curriculum, a two-stage pipeline that first builds large-scale synthetic, instruction-rich data for broad task supervision, then curates a small set of real-world data to refine generation realism; (b) Multi-Task Mosaicking, which constructs multi-task training samples by concatenating single-task examples at nearly zero cost while enabling the learned capability to generalize to non-mosaicked images; and (c) an Edit-Focused Loss, which leverages the changed regions between input and output images in synthetic data to focus training on edited regions, improving both learning efficiency and editing accuracy. With these strategies, we substantially improve both single-task and multi-task scribble-guided editing on the VIBE benchmark, achieving state-of-the-art results. We will publicly release our dataset and model.

</details>


### [133] [ControlLight: Towards Controllable, Consistent, and Generalizable Low-Light Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25569)
*Yufeng Yang,Jianzhuang Liu,Jisheng Chu,Yuqi Peng,Xianfang Zeng,Jiancheng Huang,Shifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ControlLight是一个可控、一致且可泛化的低光增强框架，通过构建大规模连续光照强度监督数据集和引入misalignment-aware加权流匹配损失，实现了对真实低光图像的连续可控增强，在保持视觉一致性的同时达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的低光增强方法通常在有限数据集上训练且只有单一增强目标，限制了在真实应用中的泛化能力和可控性。

Method: 1) 构建具有连续光照强度监督的大规模真实退化图像数据集；2) 提出misalignment-aware加权流匹配损失，以在连续增强强度下保持图像结构一致性；3) 允许用户通过灵活控制增强强度编辑真实低光图像。

Result: ControlLight在多个低光增强方法中达到SOTA性能，展现出强大的连续可控性和对真实场景的泛化能力。

Conclusion: ControlLight通过可控、一致、可泛化的框架有效解决了现有低光增强方法泛化性差和不可控的问题。

Abstract: Existing deep learning-based low-light enhancement methods are typically trained on limited datasets with single enhancement targets, which restricts their generalization ability and controllability in real-world applications. To overcome these limitations, we propose ControlLight, a controllable, consistent, and generalizable framework for low-light enhancement. We first construct a large-scale dataset of real-world degraded images with continuous illumination-strength supervision. To further ensure consistent outputs under different control strengths, we introduce a misalignment-aware weighted flow matching loss that preserves image structure across continuous enhancement strengths. ControlLight allows users to edit real-world degraded low-light images toward satisfactory enhancement results by flexibly controlling the strength while preserving visual consistency and realism. Extensive experiments show that ControlLight achieves state-of-the-art performance against existing low-light enhancement approaches while demonstrating strong continuous controllability and generalization to real-world scenarios.

</details>


### [134] [From Contrast to Consistency: Rethinking Event-based Continuous-Time Optical Flow Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25570)
*Rui Hu,Song Wu,Wen Yang,Jinjian Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于时空结构一致性（STSC）的混合监督连续时光流估计框架，结合双向互补多尺度架构和课程式混合训练策略，在多个基准上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机具有微秒级延迟和高动态范围，但现有监督学习方法受限于缺乏时序密集标注，而对比度最大化框架忽略时间连续性和结构一致性，导致复杂运动下轨迹失真。

Method: 提出时空结构一致性（STSC）范式，联合约束局部结构稳定性和轨迹连续性；设计双向互补多尺度架构；采用课程式混合训练策略，从监督点约束平滑过渡到自监督流形正则化。

Result: 在多个基准上，连续时光流估计和标准光流估计均达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: 验证了所提混合监督学习范式在连续时光流估计中的有效性，能够实现物理一致的运动估计。

Abstract: Estimating continuous optical flow is a fundamental yet challenging problem in dynamic visual perception. Event-based cameras, with microsecond latency and high dynamic range, capture brightness changes asynchronously, offering a unique opportunity to model motion with fine temporal precision. However, the scarcity of temporally dense ground-truth annotations limits the effectiveness of supervised learning, while contrast maximization (CM) frameworks, focused on sharpening the Image of Warped Events (IWE), often neglect temporal continuity and structural coherence, leading to distorted trajectories under complex motion. To overcome these challenges, we propose a hybrid-supervised framework for continuous-time optical flow estimation, grounded in the principle of Spatio-temporal Structural Consistency (STSC). This paradigm jointly enforces local structural stability and trajectory continuity, ensuring physically coherent motion across time. To further enhance representation and robustness, we design a bidirectionally complementary multi-scale architecture and employ a curriculum-guided hybrid training strategy, enabling a smooth transition from supervised point constraints to self-supervised manifold regularization. Comprehensive experiments across multiple benchmarks show that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance in both continuous-time and standard optical flow estimation, demonstrating the effectiveness of the proposed learning paradigm.

</details>


### [135] [AnE: Pushing the Reasoning Frontier of Multimodal LLMs via Anchor Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25571)
*Zehao Wang,Yihan Zeng,Zidong Gong,Yuanfan Guo,Feng Zhu,Hongzhi Zhang,Wei Zhang,Wangmeng Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出锚点进化(AnE)范式，通过真值锚定数据策展和支架剥离机制，在无需静态数据瓶颈下实现多模态大语言模型推理能力的持续提升，在8个基准上平均提升10.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SFT和RL后训练方法受限于静态数据的性能瓶颈，而自反思/自进化方法因低质量合成数据导致认知漂移和幻觉推理路径，亟需一种能忠实且稳定提升模型推理能力的新范式。

Method: 提出AnE范式，包含两个核心模块：(1) 真值锚点扩展：通过轨迹展开定位模型失败前沿，利用真值数据库检索高保真锚点进行数据策展；(2) 支架剥离机制：先通过支架增强监督锚定推理路径以降低学习复杂度和分布漂移，再通过RL剥离支架模板，将推理路径内化为模型固有能力。

Result: 在8个多模态推理基准上平均提升10.3%，达到当时最优结果。

Conclusion: AnE通过真值锚定数据策展和逐步内化推理路径的方式，有效克服了认知漂移和幻觉问题，实现了多模态大模型推理能力的忠实且稳定增长。

Abstract: Post-training via Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Reinforcement Learning (RL) is crucial for enhancing reasoning in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), yet existing paradigms often reach a performance bottleneck due to the limitations of static data. While current methods leverage self-reflection or self-evolution to push these boundaries, they still suffer from cognitive drift and hallucinated reasoning paths caused by low-quality synthetic data. To address these challenges, we propose Anchor Evolution (AnE), a new paradigm that integrates truth-anchored data curation and model evolution, achieving faithful and steady performance gains at the reasoning frontier. Specifically, we propose Truth Anchor Expansion, which pinpoints the model failing frontier via trajectory rollouts and leverages ground-truth databases to retrieve high-fidelity anchors for faithful data curation. Subsequently, we introduce the Scaffold-Stripping Mechanism to internalize reasoning capabilities. This mechanism first anchors reasoning paths via scaffold-augmented supervision to mitigate the learning complexity and distribution drift of direct SFT on raw data, then leverages RL to strip the scaffold template, thereby effectively transitioning the reasoning paths into intrinsic model capabilities. Experimental results on multimodal reasoning benchmarks show that our method substantially advances the model performance frontier, improving the base model by 10.3\% across eight multimodal benchmarks and achieving state-of-the-art results. The code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [136] [Mosaic: Compositional Multi-Concept Erasure via Vector Field Blending](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25574)
*Junseok Ko,Jungwoo Kim,Jong-Seok Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出组合式多概念擦除任务，针对流式T2I模型在复杂场景中同时移除多个目标概念的问题，构建了CoME-Bench基准，并提出了Mosaic框架，通过动态构建概念特定掩码并选择性融合，无需额外优化即可有效移除多个概念，同时保留非目标上下文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念擦除研究假设每张图像只有一个目标概念，但现代流式T2I模型可生成包含多个概念的复杂场景，这一假设已不再适用，亟需能同时移除多个目标概念的方法。

Method: 提出Mosaic框架，利用目标概念在向量场中的空间局部性，动态构建概念特定掩码，并选择性融合这些掩码，无需额外优化即可实现多概念擦除。

Result: 大量实验表明，Mosaic在复杂组合场景中能有效移除多个目标概念，同时保留非目标上下文。

Conclusion: Mosaic为流式T2I模型中的组合式多概念擦除提供了有效方案，填补了现有研究在多概念同时擦除场景下的空白。

Abstract: Concept erasure has emerged as a key research direction for ensuring safe and ethical image synthesis in Text-to-Image (T2I) models. While existing studies have explored concept erasure across multiple concepts, they typically assume only a single target concept per image, a limitation increasingly exposed by modern flow-based T2I models, which can generate complex scenes with multiple concepts simultaneously. To address this gap, we introduce compositional multi-concept erasure, a new task that aims to simultaneously remove multiple target concepts within a single scene. We propose CoME-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating compositional multi-concept erasure, which covers both intra- and cross-category scenarios. We further propose Mosaic, a novel framework for multi-concept erasure in flow-based T2I models, which exploits the spatial locality of target concepts in the vector field by dynamically constructing concept-specific masks and selectively blending them without additional optimization. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Mosaic effectively removes multiple target concepts in complex compositional scenes while preserving non-target contexts.

</details>


### [137] [Artifact Correction for Echo-Planar Imaging at Low-Field and Ultra-Low-Field MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25589)
*Sisi Qiao,Yilin Yu,Tiecheng Lin,Yuhao Liu,Jiajia Sun,Xiaoling Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对低场和超低场MRI中EPI成像的奈奎斯特鬼影问题，提出了一种无参考扫描的校正流水线，结合峰值对齐法和插值重采样策略，有效抑制鬼影伪影，提升图像质量，并扩展了低场MRI的临床适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低场和超低场MRI中，EPI成像因奇偶k空间行错位导致严重的奈奎斯特鬼影伪影，传统方法依赖参考扫描，存在局限性，需开发无参考的校正方法以降低对参考数据的依赖并提升鬼影抑制效果。

Method: 基于传统参考扫描校正方法，引入峰值对齐法无参考校正奇偶行错位，再结合插值与重采样策略进一步抑制残余伪影，在低场和超低场EPI及扩散加权EPI数据上进行了评估。

Result: 所提流水线有效减轻奈奎斯特鬼影，改善结构连续性和信号均匀性。峰值对齐法单独即可达到与参考扫描法相当的伪影抑制效果，插值与重采样进一步抑制残余伪影，实现了超低场条件下的脑结构可靠可视化。

Conclusion: 提出了一种适用于低场和超低场EPI的无参考校正流水线，结合峰值对齐法和插值重采样实现了高效的鬼影抑制，扩展了低场MRI系统的临床适用性，并为超低场EPI的DWI成像提供了理论指导和实践经验。

Abstract: Purpose: Echo-planar imaging (EPI) in low-field (LF) and ultra-low-field MRI (ULF) suffers from severe Nyquist ghost artifacts due to odd-even k-space misalignment. This study develops a reference-free artifact correction pipeline that reduces reliance on conventional reference scans while achieving improved ghost suppression. Methods: Starting from the traditional reference-scan-based ghost artifact correction method, we first introduce a peak-alignment-based ghost artifact correction method to correct odd-even line displacement without reference data. To further reduce residual artifacts, an interpolation-and-resampling strategy is applied. The combined method was evaluated using EPI and diffusion-weighted EPI data in LF and ULF. Results: The proposed pipeline effectively mitigated Nyquist ghosts, improved structural continuity, and enhanced signal uniformity. Peak-alignment-based ghost artifact correction method alone provided comparable artifact suppression to reference-scan-based ghost artifact correction method, while interpolation and resampling further suppressed residual artifacts, enabling reliable visualization of brain structures under ULF conditions. Conclusion: A practical, reference-free correction pipeline is presented for LF and ULF EPI, combining peak-alignment-based ghost artifact correction method and interpolation-resampling to achieve efficient ghost suppression and expand the clinical applicability of low-field MRI systems, providing both theoretical guidance and practical experience for ULF EPI-based DWI imaging.

</details>


### [138] [How Far Has AI Come in Liver Fibrosis Staging? A Large-Scale Real-World Dataset and Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25595)
*Yuanye Liu,Nannan Shi,Zhejia Zhang,Hanxiao Zhang,Boya Wang,Derong Yu,Nao Wang,Yuxin Jin,Yang Zhou,Kunhao Yuan,Siqi Wang,Lida Yang,Xu Qiao,Wentao Liu,Xuelei He,Xin Hong,Guoyan Zheng,Xin Chen,Guang-Zhong Yang,Le Zhang,Lei Li,Yuxin Shi,Xiahai Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍LiFS——基于MICCAI 2025 CARE-Liver挑战赛构建的大规模多中心肝纤维化分期基准数据集（610例患者，多序列MRI），系统评估了9种独立AI方法，发现最佳AI方法在特定设置下可比肩资深放射科医生、显著超越初级放射科医生，中位AI性能接近初级放射科医生水平，并揭示跨中心异质性、标签不平衡和增强序列变异性是主要挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在异质性多中心临床条件下，AI在肝纤维化分期中的真实进展从未被系统评估过，缺乏包含完整钆塞酸增强序列且有病理金标准标注的大规模基准数据集。

Method: 构建LiFS基准数据集（610例患者，多中心多扫描仪，多序列MRI，病理确认标注）；从96个注册团队中筛选9种独立开发方法进行系统评估，并与同队列放射科医生参考结果对比。

Result: 最佳AI方法在特定场景下与资深放射科医生相当且显著优于初级放射科医生；中位AI性能接近初级放射科医生；跨中心异质性、标签不平衡和对比增强序列变异性是主要挑战；空间配准、输入维度、多模态融合策略和骨干架构等设计因素影响跨中心鲁棒性。

Conclusion: LiFS为当前AI在肝纤维化分期中的水平提供了严格现实基准，并指出了限制临床可靠部署的关键挑战，为未来研究指明方向。

Abstract: Despite years of methodological progress, how far AI has come in liver fibrosis staging has never been systematically evaluated under the heterogeneous, multi-center conditions that define clinical practice. To address this gap, we introduce LiFS, a large-scale dataset and benchmark derived from the MICCAI 2025 CARE-Liver challenge, comprising 610 patients across multiple centers and scanners with multi-sequence MRI. To the best of our knowledge, LiFS is the first benchmark providing complete gadoxetic acid-enhanced sequences with histopathology-confirmed annotations from diverse real-world scanners. Through systematic evaluation of 9 independently developed methods selected from 96 registered teams against in-cohort radiologist reference results, our findings address how far current AI has progressed toward clinical-level liver fibrosis staging from three complementary perspectives. First, against radiologists, the best AI methods were broadly comparable to the senior radiologist and significantly exceeded the junior radiologist in selected settings, while median AI performance generally approached junior-radiologist levels. Second, from a data perspective, cross-center heterogeneity, label imbalance, and contrast-enhanced sequence variability emerge as the dominant challenges for AI methods. Third, from a technical perspective, methodological design choices, including spatial registration, input dimensionality, multi-modal fusion strategy, and backbone architecture, appear to modulate cross-center robustness, although no single choice alone closes the gap. Overall, LiFS provides a rigorous real-world benchmark for positioning the current state of AI in liver fibrosis staging and for enabling future research on the key challenges that limit clinically reliable deployment.

</details>


### [139] [SurfSurg6D: Geometry Consistent Dense Correspondence for Textureless Surgical Instrument Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25598)
*Daiyun Shen,Shuojue Yang,Chang Han Low,Qian Li,Mengya Xu,Qi Dou,Yueming Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对手术器械姿态估计任务中数据稀缺、遮挡严重、器械无纹理等挑战，构建了合成数据集SynSurg6D以缓解数据不足，并提出了基于密集对应的框架SurfSurg6D，在多个基准数据集上实现了优于现有方法的RGB-only姿态估计性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术器械姿态估计在自主手术机器人、技能评估和手术工作流标准化等应用中至关重要，但该任务面临高精度要求、频繁遮挡、无纹理器械、深度信息稀缺以及标注数据有限等严峻挑战，导致通用物体姿态估计方法在手术场景中表现不佳。

Method: 首先构建了新的合成数据集SynSurg6D以缓解数据短缺问题，随后提出了SurfSurg6D——一个专为手术器械姿态估计设计的密集对应框架。

Result: 在SurgRIPE、EndoVis2018和SurgPose数据集上的实验表明，SynSurg6D能够多样化姿态分布，提升现有方法的性能；SurfSurg6D在RGB-only姿态估计中优于现有方法，提供了精确且鲁棒的解决方案。

Conclusion: 所提出的合成数据集和密集对应框架有效解决了手术器械姿态估计中的数据稀缺和性能瓶颈问题，为RGB-only条件下的精确姿态估计提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Surgical instrument pose estimation provides crucial information for promising applications, including autonomous robotic surgery, skill assessment, and standardization of surgical workflow. However, this task remains highly challenging due to high precision requirements, frequent occlusions, textureless instruments, scarcity of depth information and very limited annotated data. These constraints often lead to unsatisfactory performance when employing general object pose estimation approaches to surgical scenarios. To address these issues, we first construct a new dataset SynSurg6D, to alleviate the data shortage in this task. We further propose SurfSurg6D, a dense-correspondence framework tailored for surgical instrument pose estimation. Experimental results on the SurgRIPE, EndoVis2018 and SurgPose datasets demonstrate that the introduction of our generated dataset SynSurg6D is able to diversify the pose distributions, thus enhancing the performance of existing approaches. Furthermore, SurfSurg6D outperforms existing methods, providing a robust solution for precise and efficient RGB-only pose estimation.

</details>


### [140] [UAV-OVO: Out-of-Viewpoint Generalization in UAV Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25615)
*Yu Xia,Zhengbo Zhang,Shuaihu Zhang,Zhigang Tu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UAV-OVO基准，用于评估无人机动作识别中视角偏移导致的分布外泛化问题，并进一步提出LATER方法，利用LoRA锚定进行测试时特征重中心化以缓解视角偏移。实验表明，现有模型在低视角训练后难以泛化到高视角，存在显著的ID/OOD性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机动作识别面临部署时的视角偏移问题：模型在低俯视角数据上训练，却需要在高俯视角下识别相同动作。这种偏移改变了身体可见性、运动投影和场景上下文，导致模型依赖视角相关的捷径，而现有基准未能揭示这一性能退化。

Method: 1) UAV-OVO基准：从非标定视频计算视角分数，利用视角隔离带将低俯视角视频分配至训练/ID测试集，高俯视角视频分配至OOD测试集，并确保ID/OOD测试集类别分布匹配。2) LATER方法：先用LoRA微调识别器，将学到的LoRA子空间作为语义锚点；测试时将目标域位移投影到LoRA子空间的正交补空间上再进行特征重中心化，减少视角漂移同时保留任务相关语义。

Result: 在多个代表性视频识别器上，UAV-OVO揭示了显著的ID/OOD性能差距：模型在低俯视角训练分布上拟合良好，但迁移到高俯视角时性能大幅下降，暴露了聚集精度掩盖下的视角捷径。

Conclusion: UAV-OVO和LATER共同提供了一个受控的实验平台和实用的自适应方法，用于视角鲁棒的无人机视频理解，有效缓解了视角偏移带来的分布外泛化问题。

Abstract: UAV action recognition faces a deployment shift that standard benchmarks often obscure: a model trained on UAV footage captured from low-depression viewpoints may be required to recognize the same action classes from high-depression viewpoints. While the action labels remain unchanged, this shift alters body visibility, motion projection, and scene context, encouraging models to rely on viewpoint-specific shortcuts. We introduce UAV-OVO, an Out-of-Viewpoint generalization benchmark for UAV action recognition. UAV-OVO derives view scores from uncalibrated videos, uses a view-isolation band to assign low-depression videos to the training and in-distribution test splits while reserving high-depression videos for out-of-distribution testing, and constructs ID/OOD test sets matched by class distribution so that performance differences reflect viewpoint shift rather than label imbalance. Across representative video recognizers, UAV-OVO reveals a substantial ID/OOD gap: models that fit the low-depression training distribution well often fail to transfer to held-out high-depression views, exposing viewpoint shortcuts hidden by aggregate accuracy. We further propose LATER, LoRA-Anchored Test-time Re-centering, which first adapts the recognizer with Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) and then uses the learned LoRA subspace as a semantic anchor for online feature re-centering. Specifically, LATER projects target-domain displacement onto the orthogonal complement of the LoRA subspace before re-centering features, reducing viewpoint-induced drift while preserving task-relevant semantics. Together, UAV-OVO and LATER provide a controlled testbed and a practical adaptation method for viewpoint-robust UAV video understanding.

</details>


### [141] [StreamOV: Streaming Omni-Video Understanding via Evidence-Guided Memory and Response Triggering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25621)
*Ming Xie,Zizheng Huang,Xudong Tan,Chao Wang,Xiangyu Zeng,Wenxiao Wu,Tao Chen,Limin Wang,Yanwei Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StreamOV提出了一种流式全模态视频理解框架，通过多模态证据引导的长短期记忆和隐状态驱动触发机制，实现了有界记忆下的在线音频-视觉推理与主动响应触发，并在自建基准SOVBench上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全模态方法设计为离线模式，缺乏处理持续增长的音视频上下文的长程管理机制，无法自主在适当时机发起响应；同时现有基准局限于离线单轮问答，无法评估连续多轮流式交互场景。

Method: 1) 多模态证据引导的长短期记忆（ME-LSTM），在固定预算下将历史音视频上下文压缩为紧凑的信息性证据；2) 隐状态驱动的触发机制，无需显式静音令牌或外部路由器，自主决定响应时机。

Result: 在多个流式视频和全模态视频基准上达到SOTA性能，验证了方法在在线和离线场景中的有效性。

Conclusion: StreamOV通过有界记忆和主动触发机制有效弥补了流式全模态视频理解的空白，SOVBench为后续研究提供了标准化评估平台。

Abstract: While streaming omni-video understanding demands continuous perception and proactive, real-time interaction, this crucial area remains largely under-explored. Current omni-modal methods are inherently designed for offline settings, limiting their applicability in streaming scenarios due to two fundamental flaws. First, they lack robust mechanisms to manage continuously growing audio-visual context over long horizons and cannot autonomously initiate responses at opportune moments. Second, existing benchmarks are predominantly confined to offline, single-turn question answering, failing to capture continuous, multi-turn streaming interactions. To bridge these gaps, we propose StreamOV, a novel Streaming Omni-Video understanding framework for efficient online audio-visual reasoning with bounded memory and proactive response triggering. Specifically, StreamOV introduces a multimodal evidence-guided long-short term memory that condenses historical audio-visual context into compact informative evidence under a fixed budget. It further employs a hidden-state-driven trigger to decide when to respond, avoiding explicit silence-token generation and external routers. We also curate SOVBench, the first comprehensive benchmark for online, multi-turn omni-modal evaluation. Extensive experiments show that StreamOV achieves state-of-the-art performance across diverse streaming and omni-video benchmarks, demonstrating its effectiveness for both online and offline video understanding.

</details>


### [142] [Hierarchical Consistency Learning for Test-time Adaptation in Camouflage Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25651)
*Mingfeng Zha,Tianyu Li,Guoqing Wang,Yunqiang Pei,Chaofan Qiao,Jiening Zhang,Yang Yang,Heng Tao Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出分层一致性学习（HCL）框架，通过测试时自适应、分层表示重建、任务亲和引导和原型一致性校准，解决伪装目标检测中领域刚性和标注依赖问题，在多个基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有伪装目标检测方法受限于静态训练-冻结范式，存在领域刚性和标注依赖问题，无法适应场景变化和未见过的伪装模式。

Method: 提出HCL框架，包含三个核心模块：1）分层表示重建（HRR）：通过空间重建与双流频域分解缓解特征纠缠；2）任务亲和引导（TAG）：通过通道级亲和力在分支间传播知识，缓解语义漂移；3）原型一致性校准（PCC）：将区域特征聚合为紧凑原型并建立原型-特征相似性，施加隐式和分层约束。

Result: 在四个伪装和四个水下物体检测基准上，在三种退化设置下，该方法一致优于现有最先进方法，展现出在分布偏移下的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: HCL框架有效克服了领域刚性和标注依赖，显著提升了伪装目标检测在动态场景下的适应性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Camouflaged object detection (COD) aims to localize targets that exhibit minimal perceptual differences from backgrounds through physical attributes. Existing methods, constrained by the static train-then-freeze paradigm, suffer from domain rigidity and annotation dependency, limiting their adaptability to scene variations and unseen camouflage patterns. To overcome these, we propose the hierarchical consistency learning (HCL) framework, which integrates test-time adaptation for dynamic representation recalibration. Specifically, we design the hierarchical representation reconstruction (HRR) to alleviate feature entanglement by synergizing spatial reconstruction with dual-stream frequency-domain decomposition, enhancing robustness against appearance homogenization. The pixel and spectrum inference provide structural and contextual priors. We further introduce task affinity guidance (TAG) to propagate knowledge across branches via channel-wise affinity, aligning local discriminative cues and mitigating semantic drift. To ensure semantic invariance, we formulate the prototype consistency calibration (PCC), which aggregates region features into compact prototypes and establishes prototype-feature similarity. This imposes implicit and hierarchical constraints that bridge task and representation gaps. Extensive experiments across four camouflaged and four underwater object benchmarks, under three degradation settings, demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms state-of-the-art approaches, highlighting its robustness and generalization under distribution shifts.

</details>


### [143] [ARMA-C3: A Contrastive ARMA Convolutional Framework for Unsupervised and Semi-supervised Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25657)
*VSS Tejaswi Abburi,Saurabh J. Shigwan,Nitin Kumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ARMA-C3是一个统一的非监督和半监督图学习框架，基于对比学习和图割正则化，用于生物医学图像中的节点分类。在ADNI、NIFD和三个医学成像基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法在有限监督和类别不平衡条件下取得了优于传统聚类、SOTA机器学习模型和现有图深度学习方法的结果，并展现出强大的跨模态泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学和神经退行性疾病中，由于标注数据稀缺和成像模式复杂，准确且早期的疾病识别仍然具有挑战性。现有方法忽视了样本间的相互依赖关系。

Method: 提出ARMA-C3框架，将样本/图像建模为图节点，利用对比学习和图割正则化，在非监督和半监督设置下学习结构上有意义且具有判别性的节点表示。

Result: 在ADNI、NIFD、BreastMNIST、PneumoniaMNIST和肝脏超声数据集上的二分类实验中，ARMA-C3在有限监督和严重类别不平衡条件下取得了优于或接近传统聚类、SOTA机器学习模型和现有图深度学习方法的表现，并展现出强跨模态泛化能力。

Conclusion: ARMA-C3通过捕获样本间依赖关系，在标注稀缺和类别不平衡的生物医学场景中表现优异，且能泛化至多种医学成像模态。
